{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_4af748560a394db1a0595396","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Report usage across every tool round, not just the last\n2\t\n3\tIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#93\n4\tLabels: priority:medium, type:feature, size:S, area:api\n5\t\n6\t## Why\n7\t\n8\t`afterModelResponse` is the hook a deployer reaches for when they need to record what a turn cost — an audit entry, a billing line, a usage budget. It is called once per user message, after the round loop exits, and is handed the `lastUsage` local: `chatOrchestrator.ts` assigns `lastUsage = outcome.usage ?? lastUsage` on each round, so what the hook receives is the usage of the final provider call alone.\n9\t\n10\tFor a turn that answered directly, that is the whole turn. For a turn that called tools, it is the last leg of several, and the earlier rounds — including the one carrying the customer's question and the full tool results — are not counted anywhere. A deployer billing or budgeting from this hook under-counts exactly the expensive turns, and nothing in the signature hints at it. The current behaviour is recorded in `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` so it is at least discoverable, but documenting a surprising number is weaker than not producing one.\n11\t\n12\tRaised by the reviewer on HALEngine#83, which recorded the behaviour but could not change it: that PR's ticket scoped itself to specifying the seam, not altering the orchestrator.\n13\t\n14\t## What to do\n15\t\n16\tDecide, and make the signature say the answer. Two shapes are worth weighing, and the issue should record why the other was rejected:\n17\t\n18\t- Accumulate across rounds and pass the sum, keeping one `usage` argument. Simple for the common case, and it silently changes what an existing consumer records — the number gets larger for tool-heavy turns, which is the point, but it is a behaviour change worth a MINOR bump and a changelog line that says so.\n19\t- Pass both, for example the summed total plus the per-round breakdown, so a consumer that wants provider-call granularity keeps it. More faithful, wider surface.\n20\t\n21\tWhichever is chosen, a turn that ran no tools must report exactly what it reports today, so the common path does not move.\n22\t\n23\t## Acceptance criteria\n24\t\n25\t- [ ] A turn driven through two tool rounds reports usage covering every provider call in that turn, asserted against a scripted provider whose rounds report known, different token counts\n26\t- [ ] A turn with no tool calls reports exactly what it reports today, pinned by the existing test remaining unedited\n27\t- [ ] A provider that reports no usage on some rounds does not make the total `undefined` or `NaN`: the rounds that reported are still counted, and a turn where nothing reported still reports nothing\n28\t- [ ] The type of what the hook receives makes the answer unambiguous, so a reader does not have to open the orchestrator to learn whether it is a sum\n29\t- [ ] `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` replaces its recorded under-counting line with the new behaviour, and the CHANGELOG entry states the change in what an existing consumer will record\n30\t- [ ] `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run eslint` (`--max-warnings 0`) and `npm run prettier:check` pass, with no `any` in the public API\n31\t\n32\t## Tech notes\n33\t\n34\tOut of scope: the audit entry or billing record itself, which is a consumer's concern; token accounting for anything other than the provider calls the orchestrator makes; and the `stop` chunk's own `usage` shape, which providers already populate and this issue only aggregates.\n35\t\n36\tKey files: `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` (the `lastUsage` local and the `afterModelResponse` call site), `src/types/ai.ts` (`UsageMetadata`), `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md`.\n37\t\n38\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b2bfc6af-a1f7-42d0-9820-e476a34130a6","uuid":"b1446b7d-47e4-49d5-85a9-06316cea25a2","timestamp":"2026-10-30T15:03:19.230Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/c272cbc32fd2/workspace/ticket.md","content":"# Report usage across every tool round, not just the last\n\nIssue: re-cinq/HALEngine#93\nLabels: priority:medium, type:feature, size:S, area:api\n\n## Why\n\n`afterModelResponse` is the hook a deployer reaches for when they need to record what a turn cost — an audit entry, a billing line, a usage budget. It is called once per user message, after the round loop exits, and is handed the `lastUsage` local: `chatOrchestrator.ts` assigns `lastUsage = outcome.usage ?? lastUsage` on each round, so what the hook receives is the usage of the final provider call alone.\n\nFor a turn that answered directly, that is the whole turn. For a turn that called tools, it is the last leg of several, and the earlier rounds — including the one carrying the customer's question and the full tool results — are not counted anywhere. A deployer billing or budgeting from this hook under-counts exactly the expensive turns, and nothing in the signature hints at it. The current behaviour is recorded in `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` so it is at least discoverable, but documenting a surprising number is weaker than not producing one.\n\nRaised by the reviewer on HALEngine#83, which recorded the behaviour but could not change it: that PR's ticket scoped itself to specifying the seam, not altering the orchestrator.\n\n## What to do\n\nDecide, and make the signature say the answer. Two shapes are worth weighing, and the issue should record why the other was rejected:\n\n- Accumulate across rounds and pass the sum, keeping one `usage` argument. Simple for the common case, and it silently changes what an existing consumer records — the number gets larger for tool-heavy turns, which is the point, but it is a behaviour change worth a MINOR bump and a changelog line that says so.\n- Pass both, for example the summed total plus the per-round breakdown, so a consumer that wants provider-call granularity keeps it. More faithful, wider surface.\n\nWhichever is chosen, a turn that ran no tools must report exactly what it reports today, so the common path does not move.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] A turn driven through two tool rounds reports usage covering every provider call in that turn, asserted against a scripted provider whose rounds report known, different token counts\n- [ ] A turn with no tool calls reports exactly what it reports today, pinned by the existing test remaining unedited\n- [ ] A provider that reports no usage on some rounds does not make the total `undefined` or `NaN`: the rounds that reported are still counted, and a turn where nothing reported still reports nothing\n- [ ] The type of what the hook receives makes the answer unambiguous, so a reader does not have to open the orchestrator to learn whether it is a sum\n- [ ] `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md` replaces its recorded under-counting line with the new behaviour, and the CHANGELOG entry states the change in what an existing consumer will record\n- [ ] `npm run build`, `npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run eslint` (`--max-warnings 0`) and `npm run prettier:check` pass, with no `any` in the public API\n\n## Tech notes\n\nOut of scope: the audit entry or billing record itself, which is a consumer's concern; token accounting for anything other than the provider calls the orchestrator makes; and the `stop` chunk's own `usage` shape, which providers already populate and this issue only aggregates.\n\nKey files: `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` (the `lastUsage` local and the `afterModelResponse` call site), `src/types/ai.ts` (`UsageMetadata`), `specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md`.\n\n","numLines":38,"startLine":1,"totalLines":38}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_4af748560a394db1a0595396","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_3a226765dcd64832827f8e44","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n2\t\n3\t| Field  | Value                |\n4\t| ------ | -------------------- |\n5\t| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n6\t| Status | Shipped              |\n7\t\n8\t`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n9\t\n10\t## Installing hooks through config\n11\t\n12\t- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n13\t- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n14\t- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n15\t- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n16\t- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n17\t- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n18\t\n19\t## Engine startup\n20\t\n21\t- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n22\t\n23\t## Measured semantics — out of scope\n24\t\n25\tThese two properties of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` are recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue changes none of them; each is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n26\t\n27\t- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n28\t- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L148](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L148)), and is passed the `lastUsage` local — the most recent round's usage, not a sum across tool rounds, so a deployer using the hook for billing or audit under-counts every turn that ran tools.\n29\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b2bfc6af-a1f7-42d0-9820-e476a34130a6","uuid":"aeb4d955-4c52-4a6d-81a0-8ff3b88b5737","timestamp":"2026-10-30T15:03:24.540Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/c272cbc32fd2/workspace/target/specs/hal-engine-config-hooks/spec.md","content":"# Config-Installed Orchestrator Hooks\n\n| Field  | Value                |\n| ------ | -------------------- |\n| Issue  | re-cinq/HALEngine#36 |\n| Status | Shipped              |\n\n`OrchestratorHooks` is the engine's only lifecycle seam, and `HalEngineConfig.orchestrator.hooks` is the supported way to install it: `createHalEngine` forwards the set to `createChatOrchestrator`, so a hook a deployer passes through the factory reaches the running orchestrator rather than sitting unread on the config object. This spec pins that forwarding and the measured semantics the seam already carries, so the hook-based features that follow in this epic — transparency disclosure, audit and usage recording, human oversight, provider failover — specify against real behaviour rather than against what the hook names suggest.\n\n## Installing hooks through config\n\n- A hook passed through `orchestrator.hooks` is forwarded to the orchestrator and fires ([validated by: forwards an orchestrator hook, so one passed through the config actually fires](../../src/config.test.ts#L19)).\n- An engine built with no `orchestrator` key at all still processes a message, so the field is optional in fact and not only in the type ([validated by: processes a message when the config declares no orchestrator key at all](../../src/config.test.ts#L95)).\n- A `beforeModelResponse` hook installed through config is handed the base system prompt built from `prompt`, proving the hook reaches the orchestrator and is wired into the prompt pipeline rather than only being stored on the config ([validated by: hands a config-installed beforeModelResponse hook the built base system prompt, not merely storing it](../../src/config.test.ts#L108)).\n- The value the hook returns then replaces the system prompt the provider receives; this is exercised at the orchestrator layer because `HalEngineConfig.provider` takes a `ProviderConfig` and offers no seam to inject a recording provider through config ([validated by: replaces system prompt with returned value](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L221)).\n- For a session that completes without error the hooks fire in the order `beforeSession` → `beforeUserInput` → `afterUserInput` → `beforeModelResponse` → `afterModelResponse` → `afterSession` ([validated by: fires the lifecycle hooks in documented order for a session that completes without error](../../src/config.test.ts#L132)).\n- A `beforeSession` that throws reaches the caller as a rejection, and also runs `onError` with the thrown error and then `afterSession`, because the call sits inside the same `try`/`finally` as the rest of the turn. An `afterSession` hook called under this condition can infer the session never advanced past `beforeSession`: no user-input hooks ran, no model response was generated, and no tool rounds were started — a cleanup hook that allocates in `beforeSession` and frees in `afterSession` will therefore always see the allocation and the release on the same error path ([validated by: runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.test.ts#L116)).\n\n## Engine startup\n\n- The engine's `start()` method rejects the returned promise when it cannot bind the configured port, rather than emitting an unhandled error event that would take the process down ([validated by: rejects instead of taking the process down with an unhandled error event](../../src/config.test.ts#L174)).\n\n## Measured semantics — out of scope\n\nThese two properties of `src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts` are recorded so the hook features that follow specify against real behaviour. This issue changes none of them; each is a separate, out-of-scope change owned by no issue in this epic.\n\n- The `catch` gates `onError` on `error instanceof Error` ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L150](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L150)), so a rejection that is not an `Error` — a thrown string or object — bypasses `onError` entirely while still propagating to the caller.\n- `afterModelResponse` is called once per user message after the round loop exits ([chatOrchestrator.ts#L148](../../src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts#L148)), and is passed the `lastUsage` local — the most recent round's usage, not a sum across tool rounds, so a deployer using the hook for billing or audit under-counts every turn that ran tools.\n","numLines":29,"startLine":1,"totalLines":29}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_3a226765dcd64832827f8e44","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_73f0816278ee4efd8e5eb8eb","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\n2\timport type {ToolRegistry, ToolContext, ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\n3\timport type {\n4\t  AIProvider,\n5\t  Message,\n6\t  MessageChunk,\n7\t  SendMessageParams,\n8\t  ToolCall,\n9\t  ToolResultContent,\n10\t  UsageMetadata,\n11\t} from '../types/ai.js';\n12\timport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\n13\timport type {PromptBuilder, PromptBuilderOptions} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\n14\timport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\n15\timport {toMessages, createContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\n16\timport type {ContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\n17\timport {\n18\t  shouldContinueToolLoop,\n19\t  collectText,\n20\t  buildToolCallMessages,\n21\t  collectToolCalls,\n22\t  extractStopReason,\n23\t  extractUsage,\n24\t} from './orchestratorHelpers.js';\n25\timport {appendEntry, commitStreamingEntries} from './entryMutations.js';\n26\timport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n27\t\n28\tconst DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS = 5;\n29\t\n30\texport interface OrchestratorHooks {\n31\t  beforeSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n32\t  afterSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n33\t  beforeUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>;\n34\t  afterUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<void>;\n35\t  beforeModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, systemPrompt: string) => Promise<string>;\n36\t  afterModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, responseText: string, usage?: UsageMetadata) => Promise<void>;\n37\t  onError?: (session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>;\n38\t  /** The supported seam for an EU AI Act Art. 14 human-oversight control: fires before each known tool's executor, and a returned ToolResponse replaces the call (see docs/adding-a-tool.md); it receives the model's raw tool input and, through session, the caller's authHeaders, so a policy that logs either logs personal data and credential material; the engine asserts nothing about any policy installed here. */\n39\t  beforeToolCall?: (session: ChatSession, call: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse | undefined>;\n40\t  /** Fires when the model asks for a tool round the budget refuses, after the last provider call and before afterModelResponse; a returned string reaches the user as the turn's closing text, and the engine writes none of its own (see specs/hal-engine-tool-budget/spec.md). */\n41\t  onToolBudgetExhausted?: (session: ChatSession, budget: ToolBudgetInfo) => Promise<string | undefined>;\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\texport interface ToolBudgetInfo {\n45\t  maxToolRounds: number;\n46\t  requestedTools: string[];\n47\t}\n48\t\n49\ttype BeforeToolCall = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['beforeToolCall']>;\n50\ttype OnToolBudgetExhausted = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['onToolBudgetExhausted']>;\n51\t\n52\texport const TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED = 'tool_budget_exhausted';\n53\t\n54\texport interface ChatOrchestrator {\n55\t  processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string>;\n56\t  processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk>;\n57\t}\n58\t\n59\texport interface ChatOrchestratorOptions {\n60\t  maxToolRounds?: number;\n61\t  contextConfig?: Partial<ContextConfig>;\n62\t  promptBuilderOptions?: PromptBuilderOptions;\n63\t  hooks?: OrchestratorHooks;\n64\t  sessionStore?: SessionStore;\n65\t}\n66\t\n67\tinterface ToolExecutionResult {\n68\t  clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[];\n69\t  suppressOutput: boolean;\n70\t}\n71\texport function createChatOrchestrator(\n72\t  provider: AIProvider,\n73\t  promptBuilder: PromptBuilder,\n74\t  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry,\n75\t  options?: ChatOrchestratorOptions\n76\t): ChatOrchestrator {\n77\t  const maxToolRounds = normalizeToolRounds(options?.maxToolRounds);\n78\t  const contextConfig = createContextConfig(options?.contextConfig);\n79\t  const toolInstructions = toolRegistry?.getPromptInstructions();\n80\t  const baseSystemPrompt = promptBuilder.build({...options?.promptBuilderOptions, toolInstructions});\n81\t  const tools = toolRegistry?.getDefinitions();\n82\t  const hooks = options?.hooks;\n83\t  const sessionStore = options?.sessionStore;\n84\t\n85\t  return {\n86\t    async processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string> {\n87\t      return collectText(this.processMessageStream(session));\n88\t    },\n89\t\n90\t    async *processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n91\t      try {\n92\t        if (hooks?.beforeSession) await hooks.beforeSession(session);\n93\t\n94\t        let userMessage = lastUserContent(session);\n95\t\n96\t        if (hooks?.beforeUserInput) {\n97\t          userMessage = await rewriteUserMessage(session, userMessage, hooks.beforeUserInput);\n98\t        }\n99\t\n100\t        if (hooks?.afterUserInput) await hooks.afterUserInput(session, userMessage);\n101\t\n102\t        const systemPrompt = hooks?.beforeModelResponse\n103\t          ? await hooks.beforeModelResponse(session, baseSystemPrompt)\n104\t          : baseSystemPrompt;\n105\t\n106\t        const messages: Message[] = toMessages(session.entries, contextConfig);\n107\t        let lastUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n108\t        let responseText = '';\n109\t\n110\t        // Bounded by the budget gate below, not the header: maxToolRounds executed rounds, maxToolRounds + 1 provider calls.\n111\t        for (let round = 0; ; round++) {\n112\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'starting round', {round});\n113\t          const pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = [];\n114\t\n115\t          const outcome = yield* streamRound(provider, {messages, systemPrompt, tools}, pendingToolCalls);\n116\t          responseText += outcome.text;\n117\t          lastUsage = outcome.usage ?? lastUsage;\n118\t\n119\t          if (!shouldContinueToolLoop(outcome.stopReason, pendingToolCalls, toolRegistry)) break;\n120\t          if (budgetSpent(round, maxToolRounds, pendingToolCalls)) {\n121\t            const budget = {maxToolRounds, requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)};\n122\t            responseText += yield* closeExhaustedTurn(session, budget, hooks?.onToolBudgetExhausted);\n123\t            break;\n124\t          }\n125\t\n126\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'executing tools', {tools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)});\n127\t          const {clientMessages, suppressOutput} = await executeToolCalls(\n128\t            pendingToolCalls,\n129\t            messages,\n130\t            session,\n131\t            toolCallRunner(toolRegistry!, session, hooks?.beforeToolCall)\n132\t          );\n133\t          log.info('orchestrator', 'tool execution complete', {\n134\t            clientMessageCount: clientMessages.length,\n135\t            clientMessageTypes: clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n136\t            suppressOutput,\n137\t responseText, lastUsage);\n149\t      } catch (error) {\n150\t        if (hooks?.onError && error instanceof Error) {\n151\t          await hooks.onError(session, error);\n152\t        }\n153\t        throw error;\n154\t      } finally {\n155\t        // Before both: a provider that threw mid-stream leaves an entry open, and nothing should persist it that way.\n156\t saveSession(sessionStore, session);\n161\t        }\n162\t      }\n163\t    },\n164\t  };\n165\t}\n166\t\n167\t// A negative budget means no tool rounds; a non-finite one, NaN included, falls back to the default rather than never ending.\n168\tfunction normalizeToolRounds(value: number | undefined): number {\n169\t  if (value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value)) return DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS;\n170\t  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));\n171\t}\n172\t\n173\t// Gates execution, not the provider call: a round nobody can read is never run, and the call that read the last one already has.\n174\tfunction budgetSpent(round: number, maxToolRounds: number, pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]): boolean {\n175\t  if (round < maxToolRounds) return false;\n176\t  log.warn('orchestrator', 'tool budget exhausted', {\n177\t    maxToolRounds,\n178\t    requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name),\n179\t  });\n180\t  return true;\n181\t}\n182\t\n183\t// The stop always follows the text: the round's own stop already committed its entry, and a bare text chunk would open one never committed.\n184\tasync function* closeExhaustedTurn(\n185\t  session: ChatSession,\n186\t  budget: ToolBudgetInfo,\n187\t  hook?: OnToolBudgetExhausted\n188\t): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, string> {\n189\t  const sentence = (hook ? await hook(session, budget) : undefined) ?? '';\n190\t  if (sentence !== '') yield {type: 'text', text: sentence};\n191\t  yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED};\n192\t  return sentence;\n193\t}\n194\t\n195\tinterface RoundOutcome {\n196\t  stopReason: string;\n197\t  text: string;\n198\t  usage?: UsageMetadata;\n199\t}\n200\t\n201\t// `yield*` in the caller evaluates to this return, so the round's totals come back as data.\n202\tasync function* streamRound(\n203\t  provider: AIProvider,\n204\t  request: SendMessageParams,\n205\t  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]\n206\t): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, RoundOutcome> {\n207\t  let stopReason = '';\n208\t  let text = '';\n209\t  let usage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n210\t\n211\t  for await (const chunk of provider.sendMessage(request)) {\n212\t    yield chunk;\n213\t    collectToolCalls(chunk, pendingToolCalls);\n214\t    stopReason = extractStopReason(chunk) ?? stopReason;\n215\t    usage = extractUsage(chunk) ?? usage;\n216\t    if (chunk.type === 'text') text += chunk.text;\n217\t  }\n218\t\n219\t  return {stopReason, text, usage};\n220\t}\n221\t\n222\t// A changed message is also written back to the session's last user entry.\n223\tasync function rewriteUserMessage(\n224\t  session: ChatSession,\n225\t  userMessage: string,\n226\t  hook: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>\n227\t): Promise<string> {\n228\t  const modified = await hook(session, userMessage);\n229\t  if (modified === userMessage) return userMessage;\n230\t\n231\t  updateLastUserContent(session, modified);\n232\t  return modified;\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\tfunction lastUserContent(session: ChatSession): string {\n236\t  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n237\t    const entry = session.entries[i];\n238\t    if (entry.role === 'user') return entry.content;\n239\t  }\n240\t  return '';\n241\t}\n242\t\n243\tfunction updateLastUserContent(session: ChatSession, content: string): void {\n244\t  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n245\t    const entry = session.entries[i];\n246\t    if (entry.role === 'user') {\n247\t      entry.content = content;\n248\t      return;\n249\t    }\n250\t  }\n251\t}\n252\t\n253\t// One runner per round: the policy sees each known call before its executor, and an unknown name keeps the registry's own path.\n254\tfunction toolCallRunner(toolRegistry: ToolRegistry, session: ChatSession, beforeToolCall?: BeforeToolCall) {\n255\t  const context: ToolContext = {\n256\t    userId: session.userId,\n257\t    sessionId: session.sessionId,\n258\t    workspaceId: session.workspaceId,\n259\t    authHeaders: session.authHeaders,\n260\t  };\n261\t\n262\t  return async (tc: ToolCall): Promise<ToolResponse> => {\n263\t    const declined =\n264\t      beforeToolCall && toolRegistry.has(tc.name) ? await consultPolicy(beforeToolCall, session, tc) : undefined;\n265\t    return declined ?? toolRegistry.execute(tc.name, tc.input, context);\n266\t  };\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\t// A throwing policy declines its call rather than failing the turn: every tool_use must still be answered.\n270\tasync function consultPolicy(\n271\t  policy: BeforeToolCall,\n272\t  session: ChatSession,\n273\t  tc: ToolCall\n274\t): Promise<ToolResponse | undefined> {\n275\t  try {\n276\t    return await policy(session, tc);\n277\t  } catch (error) {\n278\t    log.warn('orchestrator', 'beforeToolCall threw, so the call is declined', {\n279\t      tool: tc.name,\n280\t      errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error,\n281\t    });\n282\t    return {result: `The ${tc.name} call was declined and not performed.`};\n283\t  }\n284\t}\n285\t\n286\tasync function executeToolCalls(\n287\t  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[],\n288\t  messages: Message[],\n289\t  session: ChatSession,\n290\t  runToolCall: (tc: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse>\n291\t): Promise<ToolExecutionResult> {\n292\t  messages.push(buildToolCallMessages(pendingToolCalls));\n293\t\n294\t  const responses = await Promise.all(\n295\t    pendingToolCalls.map(async tc => {\n296\t      const response = await runToolCall(tc);\n297\t      return {tc, response};\n298\t    })\n299\t  );\n300\t\n301\t  const toolResults: ToolResultContent[] = responses.map(({tc, response}) => ({\n302\t    type: 'tool_result' as const,\n303\t    toolUseId: tc.id,\n304\t    content: response.result,\n305\t  }));\n306\t  messages.push({role: 'user', content: toolResults});\n307\t\n308\t  const rawClientMessages = responses.flatMap(({response}) => response.clientMessages ?? []);\n309\t\n310\t  return {\n311\t    clientMessages: assignEntryIndices(rawClientMessages, session),\n312\t    suppressOutput: responses.some(({response}) => response.suppressAssistantResponse === true),\n313\t  };\n314\t}\n315\t\n316\tfunction assignEntryIndices(clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[], session: ChatSession): OutgoingMessage[] {\n317\t  return clientMessages.map(msg => {\n318\t    if (msg.type !== 'entry_upsert') return msg;\n319\t    const index = appendEntry(session, msg.entry);\n320\t    return {...msg, index};\n321\t  });\n322\t}\n323\t\n324\t// A store outage is not a reason to fail the turn the customer already received.\n325\tasync function saveSession(store: SessionStore | undefined, session: ChatSession): Promise<void> {\n326\t  if (!store?.save) return;\n327\t\n328\t  try {\n329\t    await store.save(session);\n330\t  } catch (error) {\n331\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n332\t    log.error('orchestrator', 'session save failed', {sessionId: session.sessionId, error: message});\n333\t  }\n334\t}\n335\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b2bfc6af-a1f7-42d0-9820-e476a34130a6","uuid":"48e3c98e-728f-4ffe-9508-87d2108b17bc","timestamp":"2026-10-30T15:03:26.405Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/c272cbc32fd2/workspace/target/src/orchestration/chatOrchestrator.ts","content":"import type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport type {ToolRegistry, ToolContext, ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {\n  AIProvider,\n  Message,\n  MessageChunk,\n  SendMessageParams,\n  ToolCall,\n  ToolResultContent,\n  UsageMetadata,\n} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {PromptBuilder, PromptBuilderOptions} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport type {SessionStore} from '../types/sessionStore.js';\nimport {toMessages, createContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\nimport type {ContextConfig} from './conversationContext.js';\nimport {\n  shouldContinueToolLoop,\n  collectText,\n  buildToolCallMessages,\n  collectToolCalls,\n  extractStopReason,\n  extractUsage,\n} from './orchestratorHelpers.js';\nimport {appendEntry, commitStreamingEntries} from './entryMutations.js';\nimport {log} from '../shared/logger.js';\n\nconst DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS = 5;\n\nexport interface OrchestratorHooks {\n  beforeSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n  afterSession?: (session: ChatSession) => Promise<void>;\n  beforeUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>;\n  afterUserInput?: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<void>;\n  beforeModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, systemPrompt: string) => Promise<string>;\n  afterModelResponse?: (session: ChatSession, responseText: string, usage?: UsageMetadata) => Promise<void>;\n  onError?: (session: ChatSession, error: Error) => Promise<void>;\n  /** The supported seam for an EU AI Act Art. 14 human-oversight control: fires before each known tool's executor, and a returned ToolResponse replaces the call (see docs/adding-a-tool.md); it receives the model's raw tool input and, through session, the caller's authHeaders, so a policy that logs either logs personal data and credential material; the engine asserts nothing about any policy installed here. */\n  beforeToolCall?: (session: ChatSession, call: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse | undefined>;\n  /** Fires when the model asks for a tool round the budget refuses, after the last provider call and before afterModelResponse; a returned string reaches the user as the turn's closing text, and the engine writes none of its own (see specs/hal-engine-tool-budget/spec.md). */\n  onToolBudgetExhausted?: (session: ChatSession, budget: ToolBudgetInfo) => Promise<string | undefined>;\n}\n\nexport interface ToolBudgetInfo {\n  maxToolRounds: number;\n  requestedTools: string[];\n}\n\ntype BeforeToolCall = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['beforeToolCall']>;\ntype OnToolBudgetExhausted = NonNullable<OrchestratorHooks['onToolBudgetExhausted']>;\n\nexport const TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED = 'tool_budget_exhausted';\n\nexport interface ChatOrchestrator {\n  processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string>;\n  processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk>;\n}\n\nexport interface ChatOrchestratorOptions {\n  maxToolRounds?: number;\n  contextConfig?: Partial<ContextConfig>;\n  promptBuilderOptions?: PromptBuilderOptions;\n  hooks?: OrchestratorHooks;\n  sessionStore?: SessionStore;\n}\n\ninterface ToolExecutionResult {\n  clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[];\n  suppressOutput: boolean;\n}\nexport function createChatOrchestrator(\n  provider: AIProvider,\n  promptBuilder: PromptBuilder,\n  toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry,\n  options?: ChatOrchestratorOptions\n): ChatOrchestrator {\n  const maxToolRounds = normalizeToolRounds(options?.maxToolRounds);\n  const contextConfig = createContextConfig(options?.contextConfig);\n  const toolInstructions = toolRegistry?.getPromptInstructions();\n  const baseSystemPrompt = promptBuilder.build({...options?.promptBuilderOptions, toolInstructions});\n  const tools = toolRegistry?.getDefinitions();\n  const hooks = options?.hooks;\n  const sessionStore = options?.sessionStore;\n\n  return {\n    async processMessage(session: ChatSession): Promise<string> {\n      return collectText(this.processMessageStream(session));\n    },\n\n    async *processMessageStream(session: ChatSession): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      try {\n        if (hooks?.beforeSession) await hooks.beforeSession(session);\n\n        let userMessage = lastUserContent(session);\n\n        if (hooks?.beforeUserInput) {\n          userMessage = await rewriteUserMessage(session, userMessage, hooks.beforeUserInput);\n        }\n\n        if (hooks?.afterUserInput) await hooks.afterUserInput(session, userMessage);\n\n        const systemPrompt = hooks?.beforeModelResponse\n          ? await hooks.beforeModelResponse(session, baseSystemPrompt)\n          : baseSystemPrompt;\n\n        const messages: Message[] = toMessages(session.entries, contextConfig);\n        let lastUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n        let responseText = '';\n\n        // Bounded by the budget gate below, not the header: maxToolRounds executed rounds, maxToolRounds + 1 provider calls.\n        for (let round = 0; ; round++) {\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'starting round', {round});\n          const pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[] = [];\n\n          const outcome = yield* streamRound(provider, {messages, systemPrompt, tools}, pendingToolCalls);\n          responseText += outcome.text;\n          lastUsage = outcome.usage ?? lastUsage;\n\n          if (!shouldContinueToolLoop(outcome.stopReason, pendingToolCalls, toolRegistry)) break;\n          if (budgetSpent(round, maxToolRounds, pendingToolCalls)) {\n            const budget = {maxToolRounds, requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)};\n            responseText += yield* closeExhaustedTurn(session, budget, hooks?.onToolBudgetExhausted);\n            break;\n          }\n\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'executing tools', {tools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name)});\n          const {clientMessages, suppressOutput} = await executeToolCalls(\n            pendingToolCalls,\n            messages,\n            session,\n            toolCallRunner(toolRegistry!, session, hooks?.beforeToolCall)\n          );\n          log.info('orchestrator', 'tool execution complete', {\n            clientMessageCount: clientMessages.length,\n            clientMessageTypes: clientMessages.map(m => m.type),\n            suppressOutput,\n          });\n\n          if (clientMessages.length > 0) {\n            yield {type: 'tool_result' as const, clientMessages};\n          }\n\n          if (suppressOutput) {\n            yield {type: 'suppress_output' as const};\n          }\n        }\n\n        if (hooks?.afterModelResponse) await hooks.afterModelResponse(session, responseText, lastUsage);\n      } catch (error) {\n        if (hooks?.onError && error instanceof Error) {\n          await hooks.onError(session, error);\n        }\n        throw error;\n      } finally {\n        // Before both: a provider that threw mid-stream leaves an entry open, and nothing should persist it that way.\n        commitStreamingEntries(session);\n        try {\n          if (hooks?.afterSession) await hooks.afterSession(session);\n        } finally {\n          await saveSession(sessionStore, session);\n        }\n      }\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n// A negative budget means no tool rounds; a non-finite one, NaN included, falls back to the default rather than never ending.\nfunction normalizeToolRounds(value: number | undefined): number {\n  if (value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value)) return DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_ROUNDS;\n  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(value));\n}\n\n// Gates execution, not the provider call: a round nobody can read is never run, and the call that read the last one already has.\nfunction budgetSpent(round: number, maxToolRounds: number, pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]): boolean {\n  if (round < maxToolRounds) return false;\n  log.warn('orchestrator', 'tool budget exhausted', {\n    maxToolRounds,\n    requestedTools: pendingToolCalls.map(tc => tc.name),\n  });\n  return true;\n}\n\n// The stop always follows the text: the round's own stop already committed its entry, and a bare text chunk would open one never committed.\nasync function* closeExhaustedTurn(\n  session: ChatSession,\n  budget: ToolBudgetInfo,\n  hook?: OnToolBudgetExhausted\n): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, string> {\n  const sentence = (hook ? await hook(session, budget) : undefined) ?? '';\n  if (sentence !== '') yield {type: 'text', text: sentence};\n  yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: TOOL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED};\n  return sentence;\n}\n\ninterface RoundOutcome {\n  stopReason: string;\n  text: string;\n  usage?: UsageMetadata;\n}\n\n// `yield*` in the caller evaluates to this return, so the round's totals come back as data.\nasync function* streamRound(\n  provider: AIProvider,\n  request: SendMessageParams,\n  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[]\n): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk, RoundOutcome> {\n  let stopReason = '';\n  let text = '';\n  let usage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n\n  for await (const chunk of provider.sendMessage(request)) {\n    yield chunk;\n    collectToolCalls(chunk, pendingToolCalls);\n    stopReason = extractStopReason(chunk) ?? stopReason;\n    usage = extractUsage(chunk) ?? usage;\n    if (chunk.type === 'text') text += chunk.text;\n  }\n\n  return {stopReason, text, usage};\n}\n\n// A changed message is also written back to the session's last user entry.\nasync function rewriteUserMessage(\n  session: ChatSession,\n  userMessage: string,\n  hook: (session: ChatSession, userMessage: string) => Promise<string>\n): Promise<string> {\n  const modified = await hook(session, userMessage);\n  if (modified === userMessage) return userMessage;\n\n  updateLastUserContent(session, modified);\n  return modified;\n}\n\nfunction lastUserContent(session: ChatSession): string {\n  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const entry = session.entries[i];\n    if (entry.role === 'user') return entry.content;\n  }\n  return '';\n}\n\nfunction updateLastUserContent(session: ChatSession, content: string): void {\n  for (let i = session.entries.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    const entry = session.entries[i];\n    if (entry.role === 'user') {\n      entry.content = content;\n      return;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// One runner per round: the policy sees each known call before its executor, and an unknown name keeps the registry's own path.\nfunction toolCallRunner(toolRegistry: ToolRegistry, session: ChatSession, beforeToolCall?: BeforeToolCall) {\n  const context: ToolContext = {\n    userId: session.userId,\n    sessionId: session.sessionId,\n    workspaceId: session.workspaceId,\n    authHeaders: session.authHeaders,\n  };\n\n  return async (tc: ToolCall): Promise<ToolResponse> => {\n    const declined =\n      beforeToolCall && toolRegistry.has(tc.name) ? await consultPolicy(beforeToolCall, session, tc) : undefined;\n    return declined ?? toolRegistry.execute(tc.name, tc.input, context);\n  };\n}\n\n// A throwing policy declines its call rather than failing the turn: every tool_use must still be answered.\nasync function consultPolicy(\n  policy: BeforeToolCall,\n  session: ChatSession,\n  tc: ToolCall\n): Promise<ToolResponse | undefined> {\n  try {\n    return await policy(session, tc);\n  } catch (error) {\n    log.warn('orchestrator', 'beforeToolCall threw, so the call is declined', {\n      tool: tc.name,\n      errorType: error instanceof Error ? error.name : typeof error,\n    });\n    return {result: `The ${tc.name} call was declined and not performed.`};\n  }\n}\n\nasync function executeToolCalls(\n  pendingToolCalls: ToolCall[],\n  messages: Message[],\n  session: ChatSession,\n  runToolCall: (tc: ToolCall) => Promise<ToolResponse>\n): Promise<ToolExecutionResult> {\n  messages.push(buildToolCallMessages(pendingToolCalls));\n\n  const responses = await Promise.all(\n    pendingToolCalls.map(async tc => {\n      const response = await runToolCall(tc);\n      return {tc, response};\n    })\n  );\n\n  const toolResults: ToolResultContent[] = responses.map(({tc, response}) => ({\n    type: 'tool_result' as const,\n    toolUseId: tc.id,\n    content: response.result,\n  }));\n  messages.push({role: 'user', content: toolResults});\n\n  const rawClientMessages = responses.flatMap(({response}) => response.clientMessages ?? []);\n\n  return {\n    clientMessages: assignEntryIndices(rawClientMessages, session),\n    suppressOutput: responses.some(({response}) => response.suppressAssistantResponse === true),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction assignEntryIndices(clientMessages: OutgoingMessage[], session: ChatSession): OutgoingMessage[] {\n  return clientMessages.map(msg => {\n    if (msg.type !== 'entry_upsert') return msg;\n    const index = appendEntry(session, msg.entry);\n    return {...msg, index};\n  });\n}\n\n// A store outage is not a reason to fail the turn the customer already received.\nasync function saveSession(store: SessionStore | undefined, session: ChatSession): Promise<void> {\n  if (!store?.save) return;\n\n  try {\n    await store.save(session);\n  } catch (error) {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    log.error('orchestrator', 'session save failed', {sessionId: session.sessionId, error: message});\n  }\n}\n","numLines":335,"startLine":1,"totalLines":335}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_73f0816278ee4efd8e5eb8eb","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}

RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n64\t  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n65\t    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n66\t    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n67\t      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n68\t      return msg;\n69\t    },\n70\t    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n71\t    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n72\t      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n73\t      return systemPrompt;\n74\t    },\n75\t callOrder.push('onError'),\n78\t  };\n79\t\n80\t  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n81\t};\n82\t\n83\t// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\n84\tconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n85\t  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n86\t\n87\t  return {\n88\t    sent,\n89\t    provider: {\n90\t      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n91\t        sent.systemPrompt yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n95\t        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n96\t      },\n97\t      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n98\t        return {} as T;\n99\t expect(callOrder[0]).toBe('beforeSession');\n114\t    });\n115\t\n116\t    it('runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller', async () => {\n117\t      const callOrder.push('onError');\n126\t          onErrorMessage = error.message;\n127\t        },\n128\t        afterSession: async () => {\n129\t          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n130\t        },\n131\t      };\n132\t\n133\t      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('never'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n134\t      const outcome = await {createChatOrchestrator} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {OrchestratorHooks} from './chatOrchestrator.js';\nimport type {AIProvider, MessageChunk, SendMessageParams, UsageMetadata} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport {AIError} from '../types/ai.js';\nimport type {ChatSession} from '../types/session.js';\nimport {PromptBuilder} from '../infrastructure/builders/promptBuilder.js';\nimport {ToolRegistry} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {ToolResponse} from './tools/registry.js';\nimport type {OutgoingMessage} from '../types/messages.js';\nimport type {SessionEntry} from '../types/session.js';\n\n// Fixed: nothing asserts on it, and new Date() would tie the run to the clock.\nconst SENT_AT = '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z';\n\n// One expect per test needs the rejection as a value rather than a matcher.\nconst outcomeOf = (call: Promise<unknown>): Promise<string> =>\n  call.then(() => 'resolved without throwing').catch((error: Error) => error.message);\n\nfunction createSession(content: string): ChatSession {\n  return {\n    sessionId: 's1',\n    userId: 'u1',\n    entries: [{role: 'user', content, timestamp: SENT_AT}],\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createMockProvider(response: string, usage?: UsageMetadata): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    async *sendMessage(_params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      yield {type: 'text', text: response};\n      yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn', usage};\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction createErrorProvider(error: Error): AIProvider {\n  return {\n    // eslint-disable-next-line require-yield\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      throw error;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nconst promptBuilder = new PromptBuilder({identity: 'You are a test assistant.'});\n\ntype RecordedHook =\n  | 'beforeSession'\n  | 'beforeUserInput'\n  | 'afterUserInput'\n  | 'beforeModelResponse'\n  | 'afterModelResponse'\n  | 'afterSession'\n  | 'onError';\n\n// Only the named hooks are installed: one that merely exists changes the observed order.\nconst recordingHooks = (callOrder: string[], names: RecordedHook[]): OrchestratorHooks => {\n  const all: Required<Pick<OrchestratorHooks, RecordedHook>> = {\n    beforeSession: async () => void callOrder.push('beforeSession'),\n    beforeUserInput: async (_session, msg) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n      return msg;\n    },\n    afterUserInput: async () => void callOrder.push('afterUserInput'),\n    beforeModelResponse: async (_session, systemPrompt) => {\n      callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n      return systemPrompt;\n    },\n    afterModelResponse: async () => void callOrder.push('afterModelResponse'),\n    afterSession: async () => void callOrder.push('afterSession'),\n    onError: async () => void callOrder.push('onError'),\n  };\n\n  return Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, all[name]])) as OrchestratorHooks;\n};\n\n// Records what the orchestrator actually sent, for tests that assert on the request.\nconst capturingProvider = (): {provider: AIProvider; sent: {systemPrompt: string; lastUserContent: string}} => {\n  const sent = {systemPrompt: '', lastUserContent: ''};\n\n  return {\n    sent,\n    provider: {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        sent.systemPrompt = params.systemPrompt;\n        const last = params.messages[params.messages.length - 1];\n        sent.lastUserContent = typeof last.content === 'string' ? last.content : '';\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'ok'};\n        yield {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n    },\n  };\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator hooks', () => {\n  describe('beforeSession', () => {\n    it('called before anything else', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['beforeSession', 'beforeUserInput', 'beforeModelResponse']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder[0]).toBe('beforeSession');\n    });\n\n    it('runs onError then afterSession when it throws, and still rejects to the caller', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      let onErrorMessage: string | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n          throw new Error('beforeSession boom');\n        },\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          callOrder.push('onError');\n          onErrorMessage = error.message;\n        },\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('never'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, onErrorMessage, callOrder}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'beforeSession boom',\n        onErrorMessage: 'beforeSession boom',\n        callOrder: ['beforeSession', 'onError', 'afterSession'],\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterSession', () => {\n    it('called after everything completes', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, ['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hi'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder).toEqual(['afterModelResponse', 'afterSession']);\n    });\n\n    it('called even when an error occurs', async () => {\n      let afterSessionCalled = false;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async () => {},\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          afterSessionCalled = true;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, afterSessionCalled}).toEqual({outcome: 'boom', afterSessionCalled: true});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('beforeUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives the last user message content', async () => {\n      let receivedMessage = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_session, userMessage) => {\n          receivedMessage = userMessage;\n          return userMessage;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('my answer'));\n\n      expect(receivedMessage).toBe('my answer');\n    });\n\n    it('modifies user message when returning different value', async () => {\n      const {provider, sent} = capturingProvider();\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_session, _msg) => 'sanitized input',\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('raw input'));\n\n      expect(sent.lastUserContent).toBe('sanitized input');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterUserInput', () => {\n    it('receives user message after any beforeUserInput modification', async () => {\n      let afterReceived = '';\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeUserInput: async (_s, _msg) => 'modified',\n        afterUserInput: async (_s, msg) => {\n          afterReceived = msg;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok'), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('original'));\n\n      expect(afterReceived).toBe('modified');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('beforeModelResponse', () => {\n    it('replaces system prompt with returned value', async () => {\n      const {provider, sent} = capturingProvider();\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeModelResponse: async (_session, _basePrompt) => 'Custom prompt for this question',\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(sent.systemPrompt).toBe('Custom prompt for this question');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('afterModelResponse', () => {\n    it('receives collected response text and usage', async () => {\n      const usage: UsageMetadata = {inputTokens: 10, outputTokens: 5, totalTokens: 15};\n      let receivedText = '';\n      let receivedUsage: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        afterModelResponse: async (_session, text, u) => {\n          receivedText = text;\n          receivedUsage = u;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hello world', usage), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hi'));\n\n      expect({receivedText, receivedUsage}).toEqual({receivedText: 'hello world', receivedUsage: usage});\n    });\n\n    it('not called when stream throws', async () => {\n      let afterCalled = false;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        afterModelResponse: async () => {\n          afterCalled = true;\n        },\n        onError: async () => {},\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n        createErrorProvider(new AIError('fail', 'TEST')),\n        promptBuilder,\n        undefined,\n        {hooks}\n      );\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, afterCalled}).toEqual({outcome: 'fail', afterCalled: false});\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('onError', () => {\n    it('called with session and error when stream fails', async () => {\n      let receivedError: Error | undefined;\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async (_session, error) => {\n          receivedError = error;\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n        createErrorProvider(new AIError('rate limited', 'RATE_LIMITED')),\n        promptBuilder,\n        undefined,\n        {hooks}\n      );\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, receivedErrorMessage: receivedError?.message}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'rate limited',\n        receivedErrorMessage: 'rate limited',\n      });\n    });\n\n    it('error still propagates after onError hook', async () => {\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        onError: async () => {},\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('boom')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      await expect(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'))).rejects.toThrow('boom');\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('full lifecycle order', () => {\n    it('calls all hooks in correct order', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n      const usage: UsageMetadata = {inputTokens: 5, outputTokens: 3, totalTokens: 8};\n\n      const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n        beforeSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeSession');\n        },\n        beforeUserInput: async (_s, msg) => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeUserInput');\n          return msg;\n        },\n        afterUserInput: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterUserInput');\n        },\n        beforeModelResponse: async (_s, p) => {\n          callOrder.push('beforeModelResponse');\n          return p;\n        },\n        afterModelResponse: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterModelResponse');\n        },\n        afterSession: async () => {\n          callOrder.push('afterSession');\n        },\n      };\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('ok', usage), promptBuilder, undefined, {hooks});\n      await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n\n      expect(callOrder).toEqual([\n        'beforeSession',\n        'beforeUserInput',\n        'afterUserInput',\n        'beforeModelResponse',\n        'afterModelResponse',\n        'afterSession',\n      ]);\n    });\n\n    it('error path calls onError then afterSession', async () => {\n      const callOrder: string[] = [];\n\n      const hooks = recordingHooks(callOrder, [\n        'beforeSession',\n        'beforeUserInput',\n        'afterUserInput',\n        'beforeModelResponse',\n        'afterModelResponse',\n        'onError',\n        'afterSession',\n      ]);\n\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createErrorProvider(new Error('fail')), promptBuilder, undefined, {\n        hooks,\n      });\n      const outcome = await outcomeOf(orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello')));\n\n      expect({outcome, callOrder}).toEqual({\n        outcome: 'fail',\n        callOrder: [\n          'beforeSession',\n          'beforeUserInput',\n          'afterUserInput',\n          'beforeModelResponse',\n          'onError',\n          'afterSession',\n        ],\n      });\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('no hooks', () => {\n    it('works without any hooks configured', async () => {\n      const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(createMockProvider('hello back'), promptBuilder);\n      const result = await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('hello'));\n      expect(result).toBe('hello back');\n    });\n  });\n});\n\n// One scripted round per sendMessage call, to drive the tool loop round by round.\nconst scriptedProvider = (rounds: MessageChunk[][]): AIProvider => {\n  let round = 0;\n\n  return {\n    async *sendMessage(): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n      for (const chunk of rounds[round++] ?? []) yield chunk;\n    },\n    async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n      return {} as T;\n    },\n  };\n};\n\nconst registryReturning = (response: ToolResponse): ToolRegistry => {\n  const registry = new ToolRegistry();\n  registry.register(\n    {name: 'lookup', description: 'looks things up', inputSchema: {type: 'object'}},\n    async () => response\n  );\n  return registry;\n};\n\nconst TOOL_CALL: MessageChunk = {type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id: 'call-1', name: 'lookup', input: {q: 'x'}}};\nconst STOP_FOR_TOOL: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'tool_use'};\nconst STOP_DONE: MessageChunk = {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'end_turn'};\n\nconst chunksFrom = async (stream: AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk>): Promise<MessageChunk[]> => {\n  const chunks: MessageChunk[] = [];\n  for await (const chunk of stream) chunks.push(chunk);\n  return chunks;\n};\n\ndescribe('ChatOrchestrator tool loop', () => {\n  it('runs another round after a tool call and streams both rounds in order', async () => {\n    const provider = scriptedProvider([\n      [TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL],\n      [{type: 'text', text: 'the answer'}, STOP_DONE],\n    ]);\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, registryReturning({result: 'found it'}));\n\n    const chunks = await chunksFrom(orchestrator.processMessageStream(createSession('ask')));\n\n    expect(chunks.map(c => c.type)).toEqual(['tool_use', 'stop', 'text', 'stop']);\n  });\n\n  it('stops after one round when nothing asked for a tool', async () => {\n    const provider = scriptedProvider([[{type: 'text', text: 'done'}, STOP_DONE], [{type: 'text', text: 'never'}]]);\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(provider, promptBuilder, registryReturning({result: 'x'}));\n\n    const text = await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('ask'));\n\n    expect(text).toBe('done');\n  });\n\n  it('stops asking for tools once maxToolRounds is spent', async () => {\n    const alwaysTools = Array.from({length: 10}, () => [TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL]);\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider(alwaysTools),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'again'}),\n      {maxToolRounds: 2}\n    );\n\n    const chunks = await chunksFrom(orchestrator.processMessageStream(createSession('ask')));\n\n    expect(chunks.filter(c => c.type === 'tool_use')).toHaveLength(3);\n  });\n\n  it('forwards the client messages a tool returned as one tool_result chunk', async () => {\n    const forwarded: OutgoingMessage = {type: 'entry_commit', index: 7};\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider([[TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL], [STOP_DONE]]),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'r', clientMessages: [forwarded]})\n    );\n\n    const chunks = await chunksFrom(orchestrator.processMessageStream(createSession('ask')));\n\n    expect(chunks).toContainEqual({type: 'tool_result', clientMessages: [forwarded]});\n  });\n\n  it('appends an upserted entry to the session and rewrites its index to match', async () => {\n    const entry: SessionEntry = {role: 'assistant', content: 'from the tool', timestamp: SENT_AT, isStreaming: false};\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider([[TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL], [STOP_DONE]]),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'r', clientMessages: [{type: 'entry_upsert', index: 999, entry}]})\n    );\n    const session = createSession('ask');\n\n    const chunks = await chunksFrom(orchestrator.processMessageStream(session));\n\n    expect(chunks).toContainEqual({\n      type: 'tool_result',\n      clientMessages: [{type: 'entry_upsert', index: session.entries.length - 1, entry}],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it('asks for suppression when the tool says the reply is already handled', async () => {\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider([[TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL], [STOP_DONE]]),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'r', suppressAssistantResponse: true})\n    );\n\n    const chunks = await chunksFrom(orchestrator.processMessageStream(createSession('ask')));\n\n    expect(chunks.map(c => c.type)).toEqual(['tool_use', 'stop', 'suppress_output', 'stop']);\n  });\n\n  it('joins the text of every round, not only the last', async () => {\n    let seen = '';\n    const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n      afterModelResponse: async (_session, responseText) => {\n        seen = responseText;\n      },\n    };\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider([\n        [{type: 'text', text: 'first '}, TOOL_CALL, STOP_FOR_TOOL],\n        [{type: 'text', text: 'second'}, STOP_DONE],\n      ]),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'r'}),\n      {hooks}\n    );\n\n    await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('ask'));\n\n    expect(seen).toBe('first second');\n  });\n\n  it('keeps the usage an earlier round reported when a later round reports none', async () => {\n    const usage: UsageMetadata = {inputTokens: 11, outputTokens: 3, totalTokens: 14};\n    let seen: UsageMetadata | undefined;\n    const hooks: OrchestratorHooks = {\n      afterModelResponse: async (_session, _text, reported) => {\n        seen = reported;\n      },\n    };\n    const orchestrator = createChatOrchestrator(\n      scriptedProvider([\n        [TOOL_CALL, {type: 'stop', stopReason: 'tool_use', usage}],\n        [{type: 'text', text: 'done'}, STOP_DONE],\n      ]),\n      promptBuilder,\n      registryReturning({result: 'r'}),\n      {hooks}\n    );\n\n    await orchestrator.processMessage(createSession('ask'));\n\n    expect(seen).toEqual(usage);\n  });\n\n  it('resolves processMessage and makes the validation-rejection text visible to the provider on the next round', async () => {\n    let round = 0;\n    let round2Messages: unknown[] = [];\n    const validatingRegistry = new ToolRegistry();\n    validatingRegistry.register(\n      {\n        name: 'weather',\n        description: 'get weather',\n        inputSchema: {type: 'object', required: ['location'], properties: {location: {type: 'string'}}},\n      },\n      async () => 'sunny in London'\n    );\n    const provider: AIProvider = {\n      async *sendMessage(params: SendMessageParams): AsyncGenerator<MessageChunk> {\n        round++;\n        if (round === 2) round2Messages = params.messages;\n        if (round === 1) {\n          yield {type: 'tool_use', toolCall: {id: 'c1', name: 'weather', input: {}}};\n          yield STOP_FOR_TOOL;\n          return;\n        }\n        yield {type: 'text', text: 'all done'};\n        yield STOP_DONE;\n      },\n      async generateStructured<T>(): Promise<T> {\n        return {} as T;\n      },\n    };\n\n    const orchestrationResult = await createChatOrchestrator(\n      provider,\n      promptBuilder,\n      validatingRegistry\n    ).processMessage(createSession('what is the weather?'));\n\n    expect({\n      resolved: orchestrationResult,\n      hasRejectionText: /location/.test(JSON.stringify(round2Messages)),\n    }).toEqual({resolved: 'all done', hasRejectionText: true});\n  });\n});\n","numLines":591,"startLine":1,"totalLines":591}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"call_8878296ce1ff4b7c818af727","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}
